### [249] [ActiveSAM: Image-Conditional Class Pruning for Fast and Accurate Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16996)
*Tran Dinh Tien,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActiveSAM 是一种无需训练、零样本的推理框架，通过将 SAM 3 转化为主动词汇分割器，在开放词汇语义分割中实现了速度与精度的更好权衡，在八个基准上平均提升约 +1.4 mIoU，同时在大词汇数据集上速度提升高达 5.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接应用 SAM 3 进行开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）效率低下：全分辨率解码需要在整个数据集词汇表上运行，而每张图像只包含少量活跃类别子集。

Method: ActiveSAM 首先规范化和扩展类别提示，然后通过低分辨率存在预览估计图像条件下的活跃类别集合；仅对保留类别使用分桶提示复用进行全分辨率解码；预览阶段仅使用类别存在证据，跳过不必要的分割头计算；最后阶段应用边缘感知背景校准抑制低置信度像素。

Result: 在八个 OVSS 基准上，相比当前最优的 SegEarth-OV3，平均提升约 +1.4 mIoU，在大词汇数据集上运行速度提升高达 5.5 倍；在模拟真实分布偏移的图像损坏下表现出最强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ActiveSAM 无需目标数据集训练、无需权重更新、无需真值类别标签，实现了训练自由零样本开放词汇语义分割的更好速度-精度权衡，适合自动驾驶和具身 AI 等噪声输入领域部署。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) provides a strong frozen backbone for concept-prompted segmentation, but applying it directly to open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) is inefficient: full-resolution decoding is typically run over the entire dataset vocabulary, whereas each image contains only a small active subset of classes. We introduce ActiveSAM, a training-free, zero-shot inference framework that turns SAM 3 into an active-vocabulary segmenter. ActiveSAM first canonicalizes and expands class prompts, then estimates an image-conditioned active set from a low-resolution presence preview. Only the retained classes are decoded at full resolution, using bucketed prompt multiplexing with the frozen SAM 3 decoder. The preview stage uses only class-presence evidence and skips unnecessary segmentation-head computation, while the final stage applies margin-aware background calibration to suppress low-confidence pixels. ActiveSAM requires no target-dataset training, no weight updates, and no oracle class-presence labels. Across eight OVSS benchmarks, ActiveSAM improves the speed-accuracy tradeoff of training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, outperforming the current state-of-the-art SegEarth-OV3 by approximately +1.4 mIoU on average while running up to 5.5x faster on large-vocabulary datasets. ActiveSAM also demonstrates the strongest robustness under image corruption that simulates real-world distribution shift, making it well-suited for deployment in noisy-input domains such as autonomous driving and embodied AI. Code is available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/ActiveSAM.

</details>


### [250] [FusionRS: A Large-Scale RGB-Infrared Remote Sensing Dataset for Dual-Modal Vision-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17020)
*Jiaju Han,Ben Zhang,Xuemeng Sun,Qike Zhang,Yuxian Dong,Chengyin Hu,Fengyu Zhang,Yiwei Wei,Jiujiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FusionRS，首个大规模遥感RGB-红外-文本配对数据集，并基于该数据集训练双模态视觉语言模型，实验证明该数据集和方法能显著提升红外感知的跨模态对齐与描述能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型主要基于RGB图像，忽视了红外图像提供的热强度、目标边界和光照不变性等互补信息，且缺乏大规模RGB-红外-文本配对数据集。

Method: 构建FusionRS数据集，将RGB遥感图像转换为红外风格图像形成对齐的RGB-IR图像对；每对图像配有常规场景描述和红外感知描述；基于该数据集训练CLIP风格的双模态对齐模型和生成式双模态描述模型。

Result: FusionRS在RGB-IR对齐、红外到文本检索和双模态描述任务上优于仅RGB和非红外感知训练设置；消融实验证实红外感知描述对增强红外-语言对齐至关重要。

Conclusion: 红外感知的文本监督对于可扩展的RGB-红外遥感视觉语言表示学习至关重要，双模态融合能有效弥补单一RGB模态的不足。

Abstract: Remote sensing vision-language models have advanced Earth observation understanding, but most existing work remains centered on RGB imagery, leaving the complementary information in infrared data underexplored. Infrared images provide distinctive cues, including thermal intensity structures, object boundaries, and illumination-invariant scene features, which can enrich visual-language learning beyond conventional RGB observations. However, a large-scale RGB-infrared-text dataset for remote sensing vision-language modeling is still absent. To address this gap, we introduce FusionRS, the first large-scale RGB-infrared-text dataset designed for dual-modal vision-language learning in remote sensing. FusionRS is constructed by translating diverse public RGB remote sensing images into infrared-style counterparts, forming aligned RGB-IR image pairs. Each pair is associated with conventional scene captions and IR-aware captions that explicitly describe infrared-specific visual properties while preserving semantic content. Based on FusionRS, we train dual-modal vision-language foundation models for RGB-IR joint understanding. We first train CLIP-style models for RGB-IR-text alignment, and then fine-tune generative VLMs for dual-modal RGB-IR captioning. Experiments show that FusionRS improves RGB-IR alignment, infrared-to-text retrieval, and dual-modal captioning over RGB-only and non-IR-aware training settings. Ablation studies further verify that IR-aware captions are crucial for strengthening infrared-language alignment, highlighting the importance of modality-specific textual supervision for more scalable RGB-infrared remote sensing vision-language representation learning.

</details>


### [251] [MeshLoom: Feed-Forward Non-Rigid Registration of Mesh Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17027)
*Jianqi Chen,Jiraphon Yenphraphai,Xiangjun Tang,Sergey Tulyakov,Chaoyang Wang,Peter Wonka,Rameen Abdal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshLoom是一个前馈式配准网络，通过拓扑感知的编码器-解码器设计，直接从网格序列中重建顶点变形，实现秒级的多网格非刚体配准，并支持运动插值和网格变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非刚体配准方法受限于逐实例优化成本高、物体类别狭窄、仅支持成对输入或仅生成中间输出，缺乏高效且通用的解决方案。

Method: 提出拓扑感知点表示将锚点网格拓扑编码到逐顶点特征中；设计多模态编码器融合锚点网格表示与形状潜在码、图像特征等多源信号，压缩为紧凑的全局运动嵌入；轻量解码器通过查询全局嵌入恢复逐顶点变形。

Result: 在多种运动类型和物体类别上达到非刚体配准的最优结果；自然支持中间时间戳的变形生成，实现运动插值和网格变形。

Conclusion: MeshLoom通过全局嵌入-查询范式实现了高效、通用的非刚体配准，并具备运动插值与网格变形的扩展能力。

Abstract: We present MeshLoom, a feed-forward registration network that directly reconstructs vertex deformations across mesh sequences. Our approach advances non-rigid registration beyond existing models, which are typically constrained by costly per-instance optimization, narrow object categories, pairwise-only inputs, or merely intermediate outputs. The network is simple and efficient, registering multiple meshes within seconds. At its core lies a topology-aware encoder--decoder design. Specifically, we first introduce a topology-aware point representation that encodes the anchor (reference) mesh's topology into its per-vertex features. This representation strengthens the network's understanding of the anchor-mesh geometry and disambiguates points that are Euclidean-close yet geodesically distant. We then propose a multi-modal encoder that fuses this anchor-mesh representation with complementary cues from each frame, such as shape latents and image features. These multi-source signals are compressed into a compact global motion embedding that captures dense inter-frame correspondence. A lightweight decoder then queries this global embedding with the anchor-mesh point representation, retrieving per-vertex deformations at target timestamps. Through extensive experiments across diverse motions and object categories, we show that MeshLoom achieves state-of-the-art results on non-rigid registration. In addition, we find that our global embedding-then-query paradigm naturally enables the network to generate deformations at intermediate timestamps, which extends MeshLoom to motion interpolation and mesh morphing. Project page: https://meshloom.github.io/ .

</details>


### [252] [Qwen-RobotWorld Technical Report: Unifying Embodied World Modeling through Language-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17030)
*Jie Zhang,Xiaoyue Chen,Anzhe Chen,Chenxu Lv,Deqing Li,Gengze Zhou,Hang Yin,Haoqi Yuan,Haoyang Li,Jiahao Li,Jiazhao Zhang,Jingren Zhou,Kaiyuan Gao,Kun Yan,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Pei Lin,Qihang Peng,Shengming Yin,Tianhe Wu,Tianyi Yan,Xiao Xu,Yan Shu,Yanran Zhang,Ye Wang,Yi Wang,Yilei Chen,Yixian Xu,Yiyang Huang,Yuxiang Chen,Zekai Zhang,Zhendong Wang,Zhixing Lei,Zhixuan Liang,Zihao Liu,Zikai Zhou,Xiong-Hui Chen,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen-RobotWorld 是一个以自然语言为统一接口的语言条件视频世界模型，能够预测多种具身场景（机械臂操作、自动驾驶、室内导航、人机迁移）的未来视觉轨迹，通过双流 MMDiT、8.6M 视频-文本语料库和渐进式训练策略实现，在多项基准测试中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型缺乏统一的动作接口和跨具身场景的泛化能力，难以同时支持多种具身智能任务（操作、驾驶、导航、人机迁移）的物理视频预测。

Method: 三部分设计：1) 双流 MMDiT（60层扩散 Transformer）+ MLLM 动作编码，通过逐层联合注意力耦合冻结的 Qwen2.5-VL 语义与视频 VAE 潜在表示；2) 具身世界知识（EWK）语料库：8.6M 视频-文本对（2亿+帧），覆盖 20+ 具身类型和 500+ 动作类别；3) 通用+专家渐进式课程训练：先学习通用视觉先验，再在共享语言接口下注入具身特化知识。

Result: 在 EWMBench 和 DreamGen Bench 上排名第一；在 WorldModelBench 和 PBench 上超越所有开源模型；零样本分析显示在 RoboTwin-IF 上具有良好的泛化能力和多视角一致性。

Conclusion: 以自然语言为统一动作接口的视频世界模型在合成数据生成、虚拟策略评估和语言引导规划三个方向展现出应用潜力，验证了跨具身场景的统一建模范式的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Qwen-RobotWorld, a language-conditioned video world model for embodied intelligence. With natural language as a unified action interface, it predicts physically grounded future visual trajectories from current observations across robotic manipulation, autonomous driving, indoor navigation, and human-to-robot transfer. This unified formulation provides three promising application directions: synthetic data generation for policy training augmentation, scalable virtual environments for policy evaluation, and language-guided planning signals for downstream robot control. This is achieved through a three-part design: a) Double-Stream MMDiT with MLLM Action Encoding, where a 60-layer double-stream diffusion transformer couples frozen Qwen2.5-VL semantics with video-VAE latents through layer-wise joint attention; b) Embodied World Knowledge (EWK), an 8.6M video-text corpus (200M+ frames) with action-language mapping over 20+ embodiments and 500+ action categories; and c) General+Expert Progressive Curriculum, a two-stage training strategy that first learns general visual priors and then injects embodied specialization under a shared language interface. Extensive results show strong competitiveness: ranks 1st overall on EWMBench and DreamGen Bench, outperforms all open-source models on WorldModelBench and PBench. Additional zero-shot analyses on RoboTwin-IF benchmark further support robust generalization and multi-view consistency.

</details>


### [253] [The Importance of Phase in Neural Representations: An Internal Oppenheim-Lim Test of Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17037)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究了图像分类器内部是否复现了Oppenheim和Lim（1981）发现的相位/幅度不对称性。通过因果性移植实验，发现PRISM2D、GFNet、ViT-B/16中预测跟随相位/符号供体，而幅度信息可被丢弃；ResNet-50在ReLU前存在强符号编码，但ReLU和全局平均池化掩盖了这一信号。不同架构共享相位/符号身份编码，但受整流和读出几何影响以不同基表示，解释了CNN与注意力模型之间的纹理-形状差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Oppenheim和Lim（1981）发现的自然图像中相位主导身份信息、幅度贡献极小的现象，是否也存在于现代深度图像分类器的隐层表示中，并探究不同架构（CNN与Transformer）间纹理-形状偏好的机制根源。

Method: 在PRISM2D、GFNet、ViT-B/16和ResNet-50等架构中，进行因果性的相位/符号移植实验：在选定层将一张图像的相位/符号与另一张图像的幅度组合，观察分类器的预测跟随哪个供体。同时结合消融实验（删除幅度信息）和对照实验（DC-only控制），排除幅度不依赖图像的平凡情况。

Result: PRISM2D、GFNet、ViT-B/16的预测跟随相位/符号供体，删除图像特异性幅度几乎不影响准确率。ResNet-50在ReLU后移植符号无效，但在ReLU前移植发现晚期模块存在强潜在符号编码；DC-only控制表明读出层消耗的是通道级空间平均，即幅度信息被池化操作丢弃。

Conclusion: 各架构共享以相位/符号编码身份信息的机制，但整流（ReLU）和读出几何（全局平均池化）导致该编码在不同架构中以不同基暴露，这为CNN与注意力模型之间的纹理-形状偏好差异提供了机制解释。

Abstract: Oppenheim and Lim (1981) showed that natural images stay recognizable when reconstructed from their Fourier phase alone, while the magnitude carries little of their identity. We ask whether trained image classifiers reproduce this asymmetry inside their hidden layers, and we test it causally: given two images, we transplant the phase of one onto the magnitude of the other at a chosen layer and record which image the prediction follows. In PRISM2D, GFNet, and ViT-B/16 the prediction follows the phase or sign donor, and deleting all image-specific magnitude barely moves accuracy, so identity rides on phase while image-specific magnitude is largely dispensable to the readout. ResNet-50 at first seems to break the pattern, because transplanting sign after its ReLUs does nothing; a fair intervention before the ReLU reveals a strong latent sign code in the late blocks, and a DC-only control shows the readout consumes a channel-wise spatial average. Controls rule out the trivial case in which magnitude simply stops depending on the image. The architectures therefore share a phase/sign identity code but expose it in different bases, set by rectification and readout geometry, which gives a mechanistic account of the texture--shape gap between CNNs and attention models.

</details>


### [254] [BRDFusion: Physics Meets Generation for Urban Scene Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17049)
*Yi-Ruei Liu,Jie-Ying Lee,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yu-Lun Liu,Chih-Hao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BRDFusion提出统一框架，结合物理渲染与生成模型，实现城市场景的高质量、可控逆渲染与正向渲染，在真实与合成场景中优于基线，支持新视角重光照、夜间模拟和动态物体插入/编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理渲染方法存在重建与渲染伪影，生成模型虽能生成逼真视频但缺乏一致性和可控性，因此需要将两者优势互补。

Method: 统一框架BRDFusion：逆渲染阶段结合物理建模与生成先验恢复显式一致的场景属性；正向渲染阶段用生成模型去噪并修复伪影，实现可控渲染。

Result: 在真实和合成场景中均优于基线方法，生成高质量视频且支持精确控制。

Conclusion: BRDFusion证明了物理模型与生成模型互补的有效性，实现高质量、可控的正向与逆渲染，拓展至新视角重光照、夜间模拟和动态物体插入/编辑等应用。

Abstract: Inverse rendering of urban scenes from captured videos enables numerous applications, including content creation and autonomous driving simulation. Physically-based rendering methods follow and control lighting physics, but suffer from reconstruction and rendering artifacts. While generative models produce realistic videos, they offer limited consistency and controllability. We present BRDFusion, a unified framework that combines two complementary models for inverse and forward rendering. Specifically, BRDFusion recovers explicit, consistent scene properties with physical modeling and alleviates optimization ambiguity with generative priors. During forward rendering, the physical model provides controllable rendering from the scene configuration, and the generative model denoises and fixes artifacts. Therefore, our method produces high-quality videos while allowing precise control, outperforming baselines in real and synthetic scenes. Moreover, BRDFusion supports novel-view relighting, night simulation, and dynamic object insertion/editing. Project page: https://shigon255.github.io/brdfusion-page/

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [PhoneHarness: Harnessing Phone-Use Agents through Mixed GUI, CLI, and Tool Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14832)
*Chenxin Li,Zhengyao Fang,Zhengyang Tang,Pengyuan Lyu,Xingran Zhou,Xin Lai,Fei Tang,Liang Wu,Yiduo Guo,Weinong Wang,Junyi Li,Yi Zhang,Yang Ding,Huawen Shen,Sunqi Fan,Shangpin Peng,Zheng Ruan,Anran Zhang,Benyou Wang,Chengquan Zhang,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhoneHarness是一个混合行动基准测试与执行框架，用于研究手机智能体在可验证移动工作流上的表现。它整合了GUI、CLI和主机端工具操作，通过可审计的执行轨迹评估智能体是否完成任务并产生可观察的副作用。在标注评估集上达到75.0%的通过率，比最强非PhoneHarness设置高出12.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动智能体研究主要将其视为GUI控制器（观察屏幕、点击滑动），但真实的手机使用任务更广泛，需要智能体决定何时使用应用GUI、设备端命令或结构化工具，并留下任务实际发生的可验证证据。当前缺乏支持混合操作和可审计追踪的评估框架。

Method: PhoneHarness包含两个核心组件：1) 执行框架（Harness）：在设备端运行智能体循环，整合GUI、CLI和主机端工具操作，采用确定性操作路由、有界GUI委托和可审计执行轨迹；2) 基准测试（Bench）：评估智能体是否完成任务并产生可观察的副作用，而非仅生成合理的最终答案。

Result: 在标注评估集上，PhoneHarness达到75.0%的通过率，比最强的非PhoneHarness设置高出12.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 可靠的手机自动化依赖于操作表面路由和可验证执行，而不仅仅是视觉GUI控制。Harness使混合手机工作流可执行，Bench则衡量智能体是否能可靠且安全地使用该框架，二者相互依赖。

Abstract: Phone agents are increasingly expected to complete real mobile workflows rather than merely predict the next screen action. However, much of the current mobile-agent literature still evaluates agents primarily as GUI controllers that observe a screen, emit taps and swipes, and are scored by target app state. Real phone-use tasks are broader: they require deciding when to use app GUIs, device-side commands, or structured tools, while leaving evidence that the intended side effect actually occurred. We introduce PhoneHarness, a mixed-action benchmark and execution harness for studying phone-use agents on verifiable mobile workflows. PhoneHarness runs a device-side agent loop over GUI, CLI, and host-side tool actions, combining deterministic action routing with bounded GUI delegation and auditable execution traces. Its benchmark, PhoneHarness Bench, evaluates whether agents complete tasks with observable side effects, not only whether they produce plausible final answers. On the annotated evaluation split, PhoneHarness reaches a 75.0% pass rate, outperforming the strongest non-PhoneHarness settings by 12.9 percentage points. PhoneHarness and PhoneHarness Bench therefore play distinct but mutually dependent roles: the harness makes mixed phone workflows executable, while the benchmark measures whether agents can use that harness reliably and safely. Our findings suggest that reliable phone automation depends on action-surface routing and verifiable execution, not only visual GUI control.

</details>


### [256] [Evaluating the Robustness of Proof Autoformalization in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14867)
*Zhengtao Gui,Sheng Yang,Zhouxing Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了数学证明自动形式化模型的鲁棒性问题，构建了全局扰动（改写风格）和局部扰动（修改值/符号/证明步骤）两类扰动基准，评估了7个现有模型，发现它们对全局扰动敏感且大多无法忠实反映局部扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证明自动形式化评估仅关注从 curated 数据集中的规范非形式证明进行翻译，但实际应用中非形式证明可能偏离理想情况。鲁棒的自动形式化模型应在非形式证明发生扰动时仍保持忠实性，因此需要系统研究其鲁棒性。

Method: 定义两类扰动：(1) 全局扰动——以不同风格改写非形式证明，形式化结果应保持一致；(2) 局部扰动——反事实地修改值/符号/证明步骤，形式化输出应忠实反映该修改。在 miniF2F 和 MATH-500 上构建扰动基准，自动评估全局扰动下的正确性稳定性和局部扰动下的忠实性。

Result: 评估了7个近期模型，所有模型均对全局扰动敏感，且大部分模型在局部扰动下无法忠实反映修改内容。

Conclusion: 现有证明自动形式化模型在鲁棒性方面存在显著不足，无法处理非形式证明的各种扰动，需要进一步研究提升其鲁棒性。

Abstract: Proof autoformalization aims to translate a mathematical informal proof written in natural language into a formal proof in a formal language such as Lean~4. Several works have developed LLM-based models for proof autoformalization. However, existing evaluations have typically focused on translating well-formed informal proofs from curated datasets. We argue that a robust proof autoformalizer must remain faithful even for informal proofs that diverge from these idealized ones, and we present the first study on the robustness of proof autoformalization models. We formulate two categories of perturbations and evaluate robustness under each: a global perturbation paraphrases the informal proof in a different style, under which the formalization should remain consistent; a local perturbation alters a value, symbol, or proof step, possibly in a counterfactual way, and a robust formalization should faithfully reflect the perturbation rather than reverting to the original one or inferring a different one on its own. We build a benchmark with both perturbations on miniF2F and MATH-500, and automatically measure how stable a proof autoformalization's correctness is under global perturbations and how faithfully its output reflects local perturbations. We evaluate seven recent models, all of which are sensitive to global perturbations and mostly fail to remain faithful under local perturbations. Code and data are available via https://github.com/ucr-rai/robust-proof-autoformalization.

</details>


### [257] [Context Compression Is Not One Thing: Readable Symbolic Re-expression vs. Coherent Summary at Matched Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14875)
*Sisong Bei,Mikhail L. Arbuzov,Ziwei Dong,Dmitri Kalaev,Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Telegraph English——一种可读的符号化格式，将检索到的段落重写为结构化的实体-关系陈述，在更少的token成本下保留推理证据。在三个多跳QA数据集上，该方法在所有预算匹配的压缩基线中表现最优，F1提升13-20个百分点。但预先注册的深度交互假设未被验证：优势不随推理深度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对小语言模型在多跳问答中的上下文压缩问题，探索如何在有限的token预算下保留关键的推理证据，以提升模型的多跳推理能力。

Method: 提出Telegraph English格式，将检索到的自然语言段落重写为结构化的实体-关系三元组陈述，实现可读的符号化表达，在压缩token的同时保持推理证据的密度。

Result: 在MuSiQue、TwoWiki和HotpotQA三个数据集上，Telegraph English在所有匹配预算的压缩基线（字符级删除、截断、随机子采样）中均表现最优，F1提升13-20个百分点。且在最难的数据集上优于同一编码器生成的连贯散文摘要。但深度交互假设未被验证。

Conclusion: 可读的符号化重表达在匹配的token预算下比自然语言或连贯摘要更密集地保留实体内容，从而提升小模型的多跳QA性能，但优势并非随推理深度增加而增大。

Abstract: We study context compression for multi-hop question answering with small language models. We propose Telegraph English, a readable symbolic format that rewrites retrieved passages into structured entity-relation statements, preserving reasoning evidence at lower token cost. In controlled experiments on MuSiQue, TwoWiki, and HotpotQA, Telegraph English outperforms three matched-budget compression baselines (character-level deletion, truncation, and random sub-sampling) on every dataset, with gains of 13 to 20 F1 percentage point. It also outperforms a coherent prose summary produced by the same encoder on the hardest dataset. A pre-registered depth-interaction hypothesis is null: the advantage does not grow with reasoning depth within datasets. We interpret these results as evidence that readable symbolic re-expression preserves entity content more densely than either natural language or coherent summarization at matched token budget.

</details>


### [258] [Simplifying the Modeling of Arbitrary Conditionals in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14943)
*Yinhan Lu,Eric Elmoznino,Léo Gagnon,Sarthak Mittal,Tejas Kasetty,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AC-GPT，通过简单修改标准因果Transformer结构，使其能够在单次前向传播中评估和采样任意条件分布（包括过去、未来和混合上下文），同时保持标准从左到右的排序和下一个词预测目标，并支持对现有LLM进行微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因果Transformer基于自回归分解建模序列，支持高效的从左到右解码和条件似然计算，但无法高效处理任意条件分布（如给定过去和未来token的文本块）。现有方法通过新型架构解决该问题，但往往导致次优的条件建模和退化的生成质量。

Method: 提出Arbitrary Conditionals GPT (AC-GPT)，对标准因果Transformer进行简单修改，使其能在单次前向传播中评估和采样任意条件分布（包括过去、未来和混合上下文），同时保留标准从左到右排序和下一个词预测目标，兼容现有LLM的微调。

Result: 实验结果表明AC-GPT在建模任意条件分布上优于基线方法，且不降低标准从左到右的生成性能。

Conclusion: AC-GPT通过简单修改实现了对任意条件分布的高效建模，在保持标准自回归性能的同时扩展了因果Transformer的条件建模能力，且兼容现有LLM的微调，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Causal Transformers model sequences through an autoregressive factorization of the joint distribution, which enables efficient left-to-right decoding and conditional likelihood computation. However, they cannot tractably sample from or evaluate arbitrary conditionals -- e.g., a block of text conditioned on past and future tokens. Recent work aims to solve this problem through novel architectures, but they often lead to sub-optimal modeling of such conditionals and degraded generations. We propose Arbitrary Conditionals GPT (AC-GPT) which introduces a simple modification to standard causal Transformers to enable evaluating and sampling from arbitrary conditionals -- including past, future, and mixed contexts -- within a single forward pass. Unlike prior approaches, our method preserves the standard left-to-right ordering and next-token prediction objective essential for both strong performance and efficient training on natural language. Crucially, this compatibility allows existing LLMs to be fine-tuned for arbitrary conditioning. Our empirical results indicate that our method outperforms baselines on modeling arbitrary conditionals, without degrading standard left-to-right performance.

</details>


### [259] [CoRA: Confidence-Rationale Alignment for Reliable Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14961)
*Juming Xiong,Weixin Liu,Kevin Guo,Congning Ni,Junchao Zhu,Chongyu Qu,Chao Yan,Katherine Brown,Avinash Baidya,Xiang Gao,Bradley Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于GRPO的强化学习框架，通过联合奖励正确答案、承诺答案概率和基于评分标准的理由支持，来提升LLM在思维链推理中的置信度-理由对齐性，在三个数据集上将对齐误差降低最多26.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高答案置信度可能具有误导性，因为伴随的CoT理由虽然看似合理但不完整或缺乏充分支持，模型对其承诺答案的置信度与生成的理由之间缺乏对齐。

Method: 采用基于GRPO的强化学习框架，联合优化三个奖励信号：答案正确性、承诺答案概率、以及基于评分标准的理由支持度（评估理由的 grounding、连贯性、任务匹配性和与所选答案的关联性，且不向评判者泄露正确答案）。

Result: 在MedQA、MathQA和OpenBookQA三个数据集上，使用三种开源LLM，与未调优检查点、SFT和仅正确性GRPO相比，置信度-理由对齐误差降低最多26.51%，同时保持有竞争力的准确率并改善校准。

Conclusion: 可靠的CoT推理不仅需要置信度高的答案，还需要理由能够实质性地支持该答案。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning can improve LLM performance, but high answer confidence may be misleading when the accompanying CoT rationale is plausible yet incomplete or poorly supported. We study confidence--rationale alignment: whether a model's confidence in its committed answer is justified by its generated rationale. We introduce a GRPO-based reinforcement learning framework that jointly rewards answer correctness, committed-answer probability, and rubric-based rationale support, where the rubric assesses grounding, coherence, task match, and connection to the selected answer without revealing the gold answer to the judge. Across MedQA, MathQA, and OpenBookQA using three open-weight LLMs, our method reduces the confidence--rationale alignment error by up to 26.51% compared with untuned checkpoints, SFT, and correctness-only GRPO, while maintaining competitive accuracy and often improving calibration. These results show that reliable CoT reasoning requires not only confident answers, but rationales that substantively support them.

</details>

Korthikanti,Vikas Mehta,Vinay Rao,Virginia Wu,Vitaly Kurin,Vitaly Lavrukhin,Vladimir Anisimov,Vu Pham,Wanli Jiang,Wasi Uddin Ahmad,Wataru Ishihara,Wei Du,Wei Ping,Weiheng Chai,Wenliang Dai,Wesley Helmholz,Will Jennings,Will Zhu,Wojciech Prazuch,Xiaowei Ren,Xiwen Yu,Yan Breek,Yang Chen,Yang Yu,Yangyi Chen,Yaniv Galron,Yashaswi Karnati,Yejin Choi,Yev Meyer,Yi-Fu Wu,Yian Zhang,Ying Lin,Yonatan Geifman,Yonggan Fu,Youngeun Kwon,Yu Yao,Yugi Guvvla,Yuki Huang,Yunsheng Liu,Zach Moshe,Zachary Newell,Zhilin Wang,Zhiyu Li,Zhongbo Zhu,Zhuolin Yang,Zihan Liu,Zijie Yan,Zsolt-Alon Wertheimer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Nemotron 3 Ultra 是一个550B总参数、55B活跃参数的混合MoE-Mamba-Attention语言模型，在20T文本上预训练，扩展到1M上下文长度，通过SFT、RL和多教师策略蒸馏进行后训练。它采用多项关键技术，在保持与SOTA模型相当精度的同时实现约6倍推理吞吐量提升，并开源了所有checkpoints、训练数据和配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练一个同时具备SOTA精度、高推理吞吐量和超长上下文（1M token）的语言模型，以支持长时间自主运行的agent任务，并通过完全开源推动社区发展。

Method: 采用Mixture-of-Experts与Hybrid Mamba-Attention架构（550B总参数/55B活跃参数），在20T文本上预训练，扩展至1M上下文长度，后训练结合SFT、RL、多环境RLVR（Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards）以及多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD），并使用LatentMoE、多token预测（MTP）、NVFP4预训练等技术。

Result: 与公开SOTA LLMs相比，精度相当但推理吞吐量提升约6倍，支持1M token上下文长度，适用于长时间自主agent任务。

Conclusion: Nemotron 3 Ultra在精度、推理吞吐量和上下文长度方面实现了最佳平衡，特别适合长运行自主agent场景，并通过开源促进研究社区发展。

Abstract: We introduce Nemotron 3 Ultra, a 550 billion total and 55 billion active parameter Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Attention language model. We pre-trained Nemotron 3 Ultra on 20 trillion text tokens, then extended the context length to 1M tokens, and post-trained using Supervised Fine Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD). Nemotron 3 Ultra is our most capable model yet, employing multiple key technologies - LatentMoE, Multi Token Prediction (MTP), NVFP4 pre-training, multi-environment RLVR, MOPD, and reasoning budget control. Nemotron 3 Ultra achieves up to ~6x higher inference throughput as compared to state-of-the-art publicly available LLMs while attaining on-par accuracy. The state-of-the-art accuracy, high inference throughput, and 1M token context length make Nemotron 3 Ultra ideal for long-running autonomous agentic tasks. We open-source the base, post-trained, and quantized checkpoints, along with the training data and recipe on HuggingFace.

</details>


### [261] [Are Online Skill and Memory Modules Always Worth Their Tokens? A Budget-Constrained Study of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15017)
*Sina Hajimiri,Masih Aminbeidokhti,Jose Dolz,Ismail Ben Ayed,Issam H. Laradji,Spandana Gella,Nicolas Gontier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在线网络代理的增强模块（记忆、工作流、技能）在固定推理预算下，其性能优势被同等预算的基线模型（仅增加actor步骤）所超越或持平，且额外的token消耗未被充分报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在报告增强模块（记忆、工作流、技能）带来的性能提升时，常忽略其额外的测试时token消耗。本文旨在固定总推理预算下，重新评估这些在线增强方法的真实收益。

Method: 将AWM、ASI、ReasoningBank三种增强方法与一个使用相同预算进行额外actor步骤的token匹配基线进行比较，在三个模型（Gemini 3 Flash、GPT-5.4-mini、Qwen 3.6-27B）和多个WebArena域及WorkArena-L1上进行评估。

Result: 在固定预算下，基线匹配或超越了所有三种增强方法的成功率，且通常使用更少的总token。该趋势在WorkArena-L1中同样成立。不同运行间的方差对结果有实质性影响。

Conclusion: 技能和工作流记忆在特定领域可能有帮助，但其表面增益在预算匹配的actor面前往往消失。运行间方差应作为在线网络代理的核心评估指标。

Abstract: Online web agents often augment a base actor with memory, workflow, or skill modules. These modules can improve performance, but they also consume test-time tokens, a cost rarely reported alongside the actor's inference cost. We study online augmentation, where this overhead is paid on every task, and re-evaluate its benefits under a fixed total inference budget. We compare AWM, ASI, and ReasoningBank with a token-matched vanilla baseline that uses the same budget for additional actor steps. Across three WebArena domains and three models, Gemini 3 Flash, GPT-5.4-mini, and Qwen 3.6-27B, the vanilla baseline matches or surpasses all three augmentation methods in aggregate success rate while often using fewer total tokens. We observe a similar trend on WorkArena-L1 with Qwen 3.6-27B, indicating that the effect extends to enterprise knowledge-work tasks. Our results suggest that skills and workflow memory can be useful in specific domains, but their apparent gains often vanish against a budget-matched actor. We further show that run-to-run variance materially affects outcomes and should be reported as a core evaluation criterion for online web agents.

</details>


### [262] [Deep Temporal Modeling and Ensemble Fusion for Multimodal Emotion Recognition from Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15026)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Patrick Ymele-Leki,Anietie Andy,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在WESAD数据集上系统评估了LSTM、TCN和Transformer三种深度学习模型用于多模态情感识别，通过消融实验、早期融合和晚期融合策略，发现Transformer在多模态设置中表现最佳，而集成方法（融合三种架构的预测）取得了最高准确率（98.91%）和宏F1分数（98.56%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理压力与情绪识别对健康监测和情感计算至关重要，现有研究缺乏对多种深度学习模型在多模态情感识别中的系统比较，尤其是传感器融合策略（如手腕与胸部信号）的贡献尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在WESAD数据集上使用LSTM、TCN和Transformer三种深度学习模型，进行消融实验（分别使用手腕和胸部单一模态）、早期融合（在传感器层面拼接信号）和晚期融合（集成三种架构的预测结果）策略。

Result: Transformer在多模态设置中准确率最高；TCN在仅使用手腕信号时表现最佳；集成方法（晚期融合）取得了最高总体准确率（98.91±0.13%）和宏F1分数（98.56±0.17%）。

Conclusion: 传感器融合和基于集成的融合策略在构建鲁棒的生理情绪识别系统中是有效的，多模态融合及模型集成能显著提升识别性能。

Abstract: Physiological stress and emotion recognition are important for health monitoring and affective computing. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of deep learning models such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCN), and Transformer on the WESAD dataset for multimodal affect recognition using wrist and chest sensor signals. We perform ablation studies to assess the individual contributions of each modality by training models on wrist-only and chest-only inputs. In addition, we implement a late-fusion ensemble strategy that combines predictions from all three architectures trained on multimodal input. We also employ early fusion at the sensor level by concatenating wrist and chest signals before feeding them into each model. Our results show that Transformer models consistently achieve the highest accuracy in multimodal settings, while TCN models perform best in the wrist-only configuration. The ensemble method yields the highest overall accuracy (98.91 +/- 0.13%) and macro-F1 score (98.56 +/- 0.17%). These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of sensor fusion and ensemble-based fusion in developing robust systems for physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [263] [ReportQA: QA-Based Radiology Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15037)
*Yiming Shi,Shaoshuai Yang,Xi Chen,Haolin Li,Hengyu Zhang,Che Jiang,Kaiwen Wang,Xun Zhu,Dong Xie,Fei Wang,Dejing Dou,Miao Li,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReportQA，一种基于问答的放射学报告评估框架，通过构建知识树和LLM提取结构化信息生成QA对，用QAScore度量报告质量，实验表明其比现有指标更符合放射科医生判断，并揭示了当前模型在细粒度临床表示学习上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告评估指标存在两大问题：自然语言生成指标缺乏临床相关性；临床效能指标虽关注重要发现但覆盖实体有限，且因依赖人工标注难以扩展。需要一种既临床相关又灵活、支持细粒度定量分析的评估框架。

Method: 1) 收集多模态多解剖区域的数据集；2) 在放射科医生指导下构建临床实体和属性的知识树，利用LLM从原始报告中提取结构化信息；3) 从预定义模板生成QA对，通过自过滤和报告过滤进行质量控制；4) 评估时以报告为上下文，LLM作为裁判模型回答QA对，基于准确率计算QAScore。

Result: QAScore与放射科医生判断的一致性优于现有指标；实验发现当前基于报告推断的范式难以学习细粒度临床表示且存在强负先验偏差，而问题驱动的推断是更有效的替代方案。

Conclusion: ReportQA提供了临床相关、灵活且可扩展的放射学报告评估方案，通过释放知识树、结构化报告和QA对及代码促进了可复现性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Radiology report evaluation is essential for advancing automated report generation. Natural language generation metrics have limited clinical relevance. Clinical efficacy (CE) metrics evaluate important medical findings, but focus mainly on presence and cover only a limited set of entities. Due to heavy reliance on manual annotations, it is difficult for CE metrics to extend clinical entities or attributes. In clinical practice, radiology reports serve as a medium for information transfer. Clinicians use them to perform downstream diagnostic tasks without directly inspecting images. Based on this insight, we propose ReportQA, a clinical-related and flexible radiology report evaluation framework, supporting detailed quantitative analysis of radiology report generation systems. We first collect datasets covering multiple imaging modalities and anatomical regions. We then construct knowledge trees of clinical entities and attributes with radiologist guidance, and use large language models (LLMs) to extract structured information from raw reports. Next, we generate QA pairs from predefined templates and apply quality control through self-filtering and report-based filtering. During evaluation, the report is treated as context, and an LLM acts as a judge model to answer the QA pairs. Based on the resulting QA accuracy, we introduce QAScore metric. Compared with existing metrics, QAScore shows better alignment with radiologist judgments. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art vision-language models reveal that current report-based inference paradigms struggle to learn fine-grained clinical representations and exhibit strong negative prior biases. In contrast, question-driven inference provides a more effective alternative. For reproducibility and extensibility, we release the knowledge trees, structured reports, and QA pairs, along with the pipeline code for QA construction and evaluation.

</details>


### [264] [Equity with Efficiency: An Empirical Study of Tokenizers for Multilingual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15044)
*Kieron Seven Jun Wei Lee,Muhammad Reza Qorib,Andrew Ivan Soegeng,Hwee Tou Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在统一的基准上系统比较了多种公平性分词器在11种东南亚语言上的表现。结果表明，Parity-aware BPE在效率与公平性之间实现了最佳权衡，Morphology-Driven Byte Encoding在语义推理任务上表现最优但计算成本更高，而Byte Latent Transformer因架构假设与低资源数据不匹配而表现不佳。研究证明跨语言公平性与分词效率并非不可兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大语言模型（LLMs）依赖子词分词器连接离散文本与连续神经表示，但当前主流的字节级字节对编码（Byte-level BPE）分词器在结构上偏向高资源语言和拉丁语系，导致东南亚等低资源语言使用者的推理成本增加，跨语言能力差距扩大。现有研究缺乏对公平性分词器的系统比较，亟需探索兼顾效率与公平的分词方案。

Method: 在涵盖11种东南亚语言的统一基准上，对多种公平导向的分词器进行系统性对比。分析维度包括：分词器层面的压缩效率和跨语言公平性，以及下游任务性能（通过使用相同训练数据训练1.5B参数的语言模型进行评估）。

Result: 1）Parity-aware BPE位于效率-公平性帕累托前沿，以竞争性成本实现强压缩公平性；2）Morphology-Driven Byte Encoding通过形态更丰富的表示获得最佳语义推理性能，但计算成本更高；3）Byte Latent Transformer在下游任务中表现不佳，可能因其架构假设与低资源训练数据的限制不匹配。

Conclusion: 跨语言公平性与分词效率并非根本冲突，研究为设计公平的多语言模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (LLMs) depend on subword tokenization to bridge discrete text and continuous neural representation. State-of-the-art multilingual LLMs often use Byte-level Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) tokenizers that structurally favor high-resource languages and Latin scripts. For speakers of underrepresented languages, particularly those across Southeast Asia, this bias inflates inference costs and widens cross-lingual capability gaps. We present the first systematic comparison of equitable tokenizers on a unified benchmark spanning 11 Southeast Asian languages. Beyond tokenizer-level analysis of compression efficiency and cross-lingual equity, we assess downstream task performance through controlled 1.5B-parameter language model training using the same training data. Our results show that Parity-aware BPE lies on the Pareto frontier of the efficiency-equity trade-off, achieving strong compression parity at competitive cost. Morphology-Driven Byte Encoding delivers the best semantic reasoning performance through morphologically richer representations, albeit at a higher computational expense. Byte Latent Transformer underperforms on downstream tasks, possibly because its architectural assumptions misalign with the constraints of limited low-resource training data. Together, our findings demonstrate that cross-lingual fairness and tokenization efficiency are not fundamentally at odds, and offer practical guidance for designing equitable multilingual models.

</details>


### [265] [A Practical Evaluation Method for Long-Form Simultaneous Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15059)
*Yulin Xue,Siqi Ouyang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对长语音同传翻译系统（SimulS2ST）的实用评估方法，通过ASR和强制对齐获取目标语音的时间戳，再利用句子嵌入对齐器匹配源句，实现句子级延迟和质量的量化评估。实验表明该方法有效，并揭示现有系统在长语音上存在显著的延迟累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同传翻译评估主要针对短语音或预切分语音，缺乏对长语音连续输入的评估方法；先前方法难以复现且对端到端系统不适用。

Method: 对生成的目标语音运行ASR和强制对齐，恢复词级时间戳；利用句子嵌入对齐器将目标文本与对应源句匹配，实现句子级延迟和质量的指标计算（包括YAAL和xCOMET），最后聚合为系统级评分。

Result: 在代表性SimulS2ST系统上的实验验证了该方法的有效性，并发现现有系统在长语音上存在显著的延迟累积问题。

Conclusion: 所提方法为长语音同传翻译系统提供了一种实用、可复现的评估方案，揭示了当前系统在长语音场景下的关键瓶颈——延迟累积。

Abstract: Simultaneous speech-to-speech translation (SimulS2ST) enables real-time cross-lingual communication, but existing evaluation has focused largely on short or pre-segmented speech rather than long-form, continuous input. Prior approaches are difficult to reproduce and make assumptions that do not hold for end-to-end systems. We present a practical evaluation method for long-form SimulS2ST. Given source speech, pre-segmented source transcripts, and reference translations, we run automatic speech recognition (ASR) and forced alignment on the generated target speech to recover token-level timestamps, then apply a sentence-embedding-based aligner to match the target text to its corresponding source sentences. This enables sentence-level computation of latency and quality metrics, including YAAL and xCOMET, which are then aggregated into final system-level scores. Experiments on representative SimulS2ST systems show that the method is effective in practice and reveal that current systems suffer from substantial latency accumulation on long speech.

</details>


### [266] [CoCoGEC: Counterfactual Generation for Robust Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15069)
*Qianyu Wang,Xiaoman Wang,Yuanyuan Liang,Xinyuan Li,Yunshi Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoGEC，一种为语法纠错任务生成反事实样本的框架，通过改变与错误无关的上下文（词级和句级）来增强模型稳定性，在扰动数据集上取得显著提升（最高F0.5提升+20.8点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEC模型在上下文发生轻微扰动或扩展时性能急剧下降，表明模型未能理解不同上下文中的错误模式，缺乏稳定性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出CoCoGEC反事实生成框架：(1) 通过改变词级和句级上下文生成句子内和句子间反事实样本，保持原始错误模式和句法结构；(2) 通过选择标签翻转且具有高GEC互信息系数的实例来修正生成的反事实样本。

Result: 在扰动后的BEA-19*、CoNLL-14*和TEM-8*数据集上分别取得+9.9、+11.3和+20.8的绝对F0.5增益，显著优于多种数据增强基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效提升GEC模型在不同上下文下的稳定性和鲁棒性，反事实数据增强是解决GEC模型上下文敏感问题的有效手段。

Abstract: Grammatical error correction (GEC) systems are usually trained and evaluated on GEC benchmarks, but their performance often drops sharply once the surrounding context is slightly perturbed or extended. This indicates that the existing GEC models usually fail to understand the error patterns in the varying contexts. In this paper, we thoroughly investigate the counterfactuals for GEC tasks, where the subtle changes to the contexts could lead to the label flipping issue. We propose CoCoGEC, a counterfactual generation framework that creates copies of training instances with error-irrelevant contexts altered. Our framework systematically generates counterfactuals by (1) generating intra- and inter-sentence counterfactuals that maintain the error patterns as well as syntax of the original instances by altering the word-level and sentence-level contexts; (2) revising the generated counterfactuals by selecting the instances with flipped labels and high GEC Mutual Information (MI) coefficient. Extensive experiments show that our method substantially improves the stability of GEC models, outperforming a set of data augmentation baselines. Particularly, it could achieve absolute F0.5 gains of +9.9, +11.3, and +20.8 points on the perturbed BEA-19*,CoNLL-14*, and TEM-8* data set.Our code is released at https://github.com/Quinnok/CoCoGEC

</details>


### [267] [Stop When Further Reasoning Won't Help: Attention-State Adaptive Generation in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15070)
*Jiakai Li,Ke Qin,Rongzheng Wang,Yizhuo Ma,Qizhi Chen,Muquan Li,Shuang Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASAG方法，通过注意力分布推断模型推理状态并自适应调整生成策略，在不需训练的情况下实现早停，从而减少大推理模型（LRMs）的过度思考问题。在九个基准测试中，该方法将Qwen3-8B的平均准确率提升3.2%，同时生成token数量减少近40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）虽然通过显式思维链（CoT）能解决复杂问题，但普遍存在过度思考（overthinking）问题，导致冗余token输出和准确率下降。现有缓解方法存在限制：基于训练的方法计算资源需求大，而免训练方法依赖精心设计的提示词或不可靠的置信度信号。

Method: 提出ASAG（Attention State Adaptive Generation）框架，从注意力分布角度推断模型的推理状态，据此自适应调整生成策略。该方法无需训练，即插即用，可无缝集成到现有LRMs中。

Result: 在九个基准测试上的大量实验表明，ASAG在多种主流LRM（包括DeepSeek-R1-Distill和Qwen3系列的不同参数规模）上均取得一致改进。具体地，在Qwen3-8B上，ASAG将平均准确率提升3.2%，同时生成token数量减少近40%。

Conclusion: ASAG通过注意力分布推断实现早停，是一种有效、免训练且即插即用的过度思考缓解方法，显著提升了LRMs的效率与准确性。

Abstract: By incorporating test-time compute scaling, large reasoning models (LRMs) can solve complex problems through explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning processes. However, they often suffer from overthinking, resulting in redundant token outputs and degraded accuracy. Current methods to mitigate this issue remain limited: training-based approaches require substantial computational resources, while training-free methods rely on well-crafted prompts or unreliable confidence signals. In this work, we investigate early stopping from the perspective of attention distributions and propose a simple method, ASAG, which infers the model's reasoning state and adaptively adjusts the generation strategy. The proposed framework is training-free and plug-and-play, enabling seamless integration into existing LRMs. Extensive experiments on nine benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements across mainstream LRMs with varying parameter scales, including the DeepSeek-R1-Distill and Qwen3 series. Specifically, ASAG improves average accuracy by 3.2% while reducing the number of generated tokens by nearly 40% across all reasoning tasks on Qwen3-8B.

</details>


### [268] [Ling and Ring 2.6 Technical Report: Efficient and Instant Agentic Intelligence at Trillion-Parameter Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15079)
*Ang Li,Ben Liu,Bin Han,Bin Hu,Bin Jing,Binbin Hu,Bing Li,Cai Chen,Caizhi Tang,Changxin Tian,Chao Huang,Chao Zhang,Chen Liang,Chen Qian,Chengfu Tang,Chengyao Wen,Chilin Fu,Chunwei Wu,Cong Zhang,Cunyin Peng,Daixin Wang,Dalong Zhang,Deng Zhao,Dingnan Jin,Dingyuan Zhu,Donghao Zhang,Fan Yuan,Fangzheng Zhao,Fanzhuang Meng,Feifan Wu,Feng Xu,Fengbin Fang,Gangshan Wang,Guodong Yang,Hailin Zhao,Haitao Wang,Haitao Zhang,Hanxiao Zhang,Hanzi Wang,Hao Dai,Hao Liu,Hao Qian,Hao Wu,Haoxiong Liu,Haoyu Xu,Heng Zhang,Hong Liu,Hongliang Zhang,Hongrui Liu,Hongxun Li,Hongzhi Ruan,Huaidong Xiong,Huihuang Zheng,Huikang Tang,Jia Guo,Jia Li,Jia Liu,Jiameng Wang,Jiaming Liu,Jiannan Shi,Jianping Wei,Jiaolong Yang,Jiapeng Wang,Jie Gao,Jie Wang,Jiewei Wu,Jin Yang,Jinjin Li,Jinjing Huang,Jinquan Sun,Jinyao Chen,Juanhui Tu,Jun Liu,Jun Mei,Jun Xu,Jun Zhou,Junjie Ou,Junnan Sipan,Junpeng Fang,Kaihong Zhang,Kaiqin Hu,Ke Shi,Kuan Xu,Kun Tang,Kunlong Chen,Lanyin Mei,Lei Chen,Lei Liang,Lei Xu,Li Tang,Liang Jiang,Liangcheng Fu,Lihui Zhang,Linfeng Shi,Lintao Ma,Liyuan Liu,Longfei Li,Longfei Zheng,Lu Liu,Lu Yu,Man Li,Meiqi Zhu,Meng Li,Mengjie Gao,Mengshu Sun,Mingming Yin,Mingyang Zhang,Mingyuan Fan,Nuo Xu,Pan Tang,Peijie Jiang,Peilong Zhao,Peng Lin,Pingping Liu,Qi Zuo,Qian Zhao,Qiang Cheng,Qianggang Cao,Qiaoben Bao,Qing Cui,Qingyuan Yang,Qitao Shi,Qiyin Huang,Qizheng Zhou,Quan Wan,Runyuan Zhao,Shaomian Zheng,Shaowei Wei,Shengnan Zhang,Shuaicheng Li,Shujie Li,Shuo Zhang,Sikang Bian,Tianchu Yao,Tiange Xu,Tianshu Wang,Ting Guo,Tinghao Wang,Tingwei Huang,Tong Zhao,Tongkai Yang,Wang Hong,Wanli Gu,Wei Lu,Weichang Wu,Weiguang Han,Weiquan Li,Wenbo Shen,Wenjing Fang,Wenzhi Tang,Xiang Shu,Xiao Shi,Xiaodong Yan,Xiaolu Zhang,Xiaopei Wan,Xiaqing Sun,Xin Zhao,Xingyu Lu,Xinxing Yang,Xinyao Tang,Xinyu Kong,Xinyu Liu,Xiong Xu,Xuan Sun,Xudong Han,Xudong Wang,Xujie Shen,Yalin Zhang,Yangyang Hou,Yankun Ren,Yao Zhao,Ye Chen,Yeyang Chen,Yibo Cao,Yifan Zuo,Yijie Chen,Ying Li,Yingjie Song,Yingxue Li,Yiqi Wang,Yixuan Sun,Yizhu Xiao,Yongfei Xu,Yu Liu,Yuchen Fang,Yue Gao,Yue Yu,Yue Zhang,Yuqi Zhang,Yuxiao He,Yuxiao Lu,Yuxin Tian,Yuxuan Li,Yuzhuo Fu,Zhankai Xu,Zhaoxin Huan,Zhenduo Zhang,Zhengke Gui,Zhengyu Huang,Zhenjun Ma,Zhenxuan Pan,Zheping Qu,Zhibo Zhu,Zhidong Fan,Zhigang Huangfu,Zhihao Wang,Zhiqiang Zhang,Zhizhen Liu,Zhuyan Zhou,Zibin Lin,Zihang Zeng,Zihao Wang,Zilong Wang,Ziqi Liu,Zitao Xuan,Zixuan Cheng,Zujie Wen,Zuoli Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Ling-2.6和Ring-2.6模型族，通过架构迁移预训练和统一协同设计，在轻量级和深度推理两个方向上实现高效、可扩展的智能体系统。Ling-2.6侧重低延迟高能力密度，Ring-2.6侧重复杂推理与智能体工作流。核心创新包括混合线性注意力（Lightning Attention+MLA）、进化思维链、语言单元策略优化、最短正确响应蒸馏，以及面向大规模环境交互数据的KPop强化学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体模型难以同时满足低延迟响应、强推理能力和实际部署效率的需求。从头训练大模型成本高昂且周期长，因此需要在现有基座模型基础上进行高效升级，实现能力与部署效率的统一提升。

Method: 架构层面引入Lightning Attention与MLA混合的线性注意力设计；训练层面采用进化思维链、语言单元策略优化、双向偏好对齐和最短正确响应蒸馏；智能体能力方面提出KPop框架（异步调度编码、搜索、工具使用和工作流执行）进行大规模环境交互强化学习训练。

Result: 构建了Ling-2.6（即时响应优化）和Ring-2.6（深度推理优化）两个模型族，其中Ring-2.6-1T成功利用KPop在大规模环境数据上稳定训练，实现编码、搜索、工具使用和工作流执行的高效异步调度。

Conclusion: Ling-2.6和Ring-2.6提供了一条面向高效、可扩展、开放的智能体系统的实用路径，所有2.6族检查点已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Efficient and scalable agentic intelligence requires models that can deliver both low-latency responses and strong reasoning capabilities while remaining practical to train, serve, and deploy. In this report, we present Ling-2.6 and Ring-2.6, a family of models designed to address this challenge at scale. Ling-2.6 is optimized for instant response generation and high capability per output token, whereas Ring-2.6 is tailored for deeper reasoning and more advanced agentic workflows. Instead of training from scratch, we upgrade the Ling-2.0 base model through architectural migration pre-training and large-scale post-training. This upgrade is guided by a unified co-design of model architecture, optimization objectives, serving systems, and agent training environments, enabling improvements in both model capability and deployment efficiency. At the architectural level, we introduce a hybrid linear attention design that integrates Lightning Attention with MLA, improving the efficiency of long-context training and decoding. To further enhance token efficiency, we optimize capability per output token through Evolutionary Chain-of-Thought, Linguistic Unit Policy Optimization, bidirectional preference alignment, and shortest-correct-response distillation. For agentic capabilities, we propose KPop, a reinforcement learning framework designed to support stable training of Ring-2.6-1T on large-scale environment-grounded data. KPop improves training efficiency through asynchronous scheduling across coding, search, tool use, and workflow execution, enabling scalable learning from complex agent-environment interactions. Together, Ling-2.6 and Ring-2.6 provide a practical pathway toward efficient, scalable, and open agentic systems. We open-source all checkpoints in the 2.6 family to support further research and development in practical agentic intelligence.

</details>


### [269] [AdaMame: A Training Recipe for Adaptive Multilingual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15080)
*Dayeon Ki,Kevin Duh,Marine Carpuat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaMame提出两阶段训练方法（SFT+自适应GRPO），解决大推理模型在多语言数学推理中的语言崩溃问题，在准确性、语言忠实度和令牌效率上实现帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM在非英语查询中常出现语言崩溃（用英语而非查询语言推理），基于RL的修复方法在加入语言忠实度奖励后仍存在准确性下降、推理中途语码转换和令牌浪费等问题。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段SFT在五种语言的自然推理轨迹上微调建立多语言推理能力；第二阶段提出AdaMame-GRPO，引入查询条件对齐因子并在训练中渐进增长，引导模型先探索多样推理语言，后利用查询语言推理。

Result: 在两个基准、两个LRM和12种语言上，AdaMame-GRPO在所有基线上实现推理准确性、语言忠实度和令牌效率的帕累托最优，在域外低资源语言上提升最显著。

Conclusion: AdaMame通过自适应渐进对齐策略有效解决了多语言推理中的语言崩溃与准确性折衷问题，尤其对低资源语言表现出强泛化能力。

Abstract: While Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) show strong performance in English, they often fail to reason in the language of the query, a phenomenon known as language collapse. Existing RL-based fixes typically add a binary language fidelity reward to the accuracy objective, yet still incur trade-off in accuracy, mid-trace code-switching, and excessive token usage. In this work, we propose AdaMame, a two-stage training recipe for multilingual mathematical reasoning that addresses these limitations by adaptively aligning the reasoning language to the query language without compromising accuracy. The first SFT stage fine-tunes on naturally occurring reasoning traces across five languages to establish multilingual reasoning capability. In the subsequent RL stage, we introduce AdaMame-GRPO, an adaptation of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) in which a query-conditioned alignment factor grows progressively during training, guiding the model to first explore diverse reasoning languages before exploiting reasoning in the query language. Evaluated across two benchmarks, two LRMs, and 12 languages, AdaMame-GRPO achieves Pareto-optimal performance across reasoning accuracy, language fidelity, and token efficiency over all baselines, with the strongest gains on out-of-domain, lower-resource languages.

</details>


### [270] [When Cognitive Graphs Meet LLMs: BDEI Cognitive Pathways for Panic Emotional Arousal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15121)
*Mengzhu Liu,Long Qin,Chuan Ai,Zhengqiu Zhu,Hongru Liang,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Xin Lu,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PanicCognitivePath (PCP)框架，通过心理安全距离模型融合多域信号、引入显式情绪节点形成BDEI路径、限制LLM作用范围，实现了恐慌情绪唤醒时机的精准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未显式建模情绪唤醒过程，无法预测个体恐慌情绪唤醒时机，亟需一种基于评价情绪理论的可预测框架。

Method: (1) 基于心理距离理论构建PSD模型，将四域信号映射为统一风险度量；(2) 在BDI模型中引入显式情绪节点，形成BDEI认知路径；(3) 将LLM限制为仅估计信念到欲望的单步参数，防止错误传播。

Result: 在飓风桑迪数据集上，唤醒时机准确率提升10.68%，峰值计数误差降至7.07%。

Conclusion: PCP通过融合评价情绪理论与认知架构，有效解决了情绪唤醒时序预测问题，验证了心理距离与评价情绪理论在应急干预中的价值。

Abstract: Predicting individual panic emotional arousal timing before manifestation is essential for proactive emergency intervention. Existing methods incorporate cognitive elements but none explicitly model the emotional arousal process, making them ill-suited for emotional arousal timing prediction. We argue that grounding prediction in appraisal emotion theory is necessary because it explicitly models this process, but three problems must be solved. (1) Appraisal theory posits that emotion arises from simultaneous evaluation across multiple threat dimensions, yet no prior work fuses these inputs into risk perception. (2) Existing cognitive models lack an Emotion node, decoupling threat appraisal from emotional arousal and forcing emotions to be inferred indirectly from behaviors. (3) Given their generalizable cognitive reasoning, current approaches adopt LLMs as the primary decision-maker, yet overlook the fragility and hallucination-proneness of their outputs. To address these issues, we introduce PanicCognitivePath (PCP), a framework that addresses all three. A Psychological Safety Distance (PSD) model, grounded in psychological distance theory, maps four-domain signals into a unified risk metric as the entry condition for subsequent cognitive reasoning. An explicit Emotion node grounded in appraisal emotion theory is introduced into BDI, forming a Belief-Desire-Emotion-Intention (BDEI) pathway. Agents whose risk metric exceeds the PSD threshold enter this pathway, coupling threat appraisal directly to emotional arousal. The BDEI pathway governs all state transitions while the LLM is confined to parameter estimation for the Belief-to-Desire transition, confining hallucinations to a single step and preventing error propagation. Experiments on Hurricane Sandy show PCP improves arousal timing accuracy by 10.68% over baselines, reduces peak count error to 7.07%.

</details>


### [271] [PACUTE: Phonology-, Affix-, and Character-level Understanding of Tokens for Filipino](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15144)
*Jann Railey Montalan,David Demitri Africa,Jimson Paulo Layacan,Richell Isaiah Flores,Ivan Yuri De Leon,Lance Calvin Gamboa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PACUTE基准测试，包含4,600个任务，用于评估大语言模型对菲律宾语形态结构的理解能力。研究发现，开源模型在语素分解上接近随机水平，前沿模型虽表现更好，但在语素变换和音节划分等组合任务上仍远低于字符级上限，揭示了形态组合能力是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型以子词令牌处理文本，掩盖了字符级和形态学结构，尤其对非连接形态语言（如菲律宾语）影响严重，标准分词器系统性地错位令牌边界与语素边界。

Method: 构建PACUTE基准（4,600个任务），包含6个组合层次的诊断框架，用于定位形态理解失效的层级；评估开源和前沿商业模型。

Result: 开源模型在语素分解上接近随机；前沿模型在包含匹配评分下能恢复单个词缀，但在语素变换和音节划分等组合任务上远低于字符级上限。

Conclusion: 菲律宾语词汇结构理解的核心瓶颈在于生产性形态组合能力，而非字符访问能力本身。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) process text as sequences of subword tokens, which can obscure the character-level and morphological structure that underlies word formation. This limitation is most acute for languages with non-concatenative morphology, where standard tokenizers systematically misalign token boundaries with morpheme boundaries. We introduce PACUTE, a diagnostic benchmark of 4,600 tasks designed to evaluate morphological understanding in Filipino, a language characterized by productive infixation, reduplication, and diacritic-driven lexical distinctions that are typically absent from written text. PACUTE includes a hierarchical diagnostic framework of six compositional levels that localizes where morphological understanding breaks down. Evaluating open-weight LLMs and frontier commercial models, we find that open-weight models perform near chance on morpheme decomposition regardless of scale. Frontier models perform much better, often recovering individual affixes under contains-match scoring, but remain far below their character-level ceilings on compositional tasks of morpheme transformations and syllabification. These results identify productive morphological composition, rather than character access alone, as the persistent bottleneck for Filipino word-structure understanding.

</details>


### [272] [Can Agents Read the Room? Benchmarking Visual Social Intelligence in Multimodal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15152)
*Shijun Wan,Xuehai Wu,Jiwen Zhang,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentViSS，一个评估多模态社交智能的基准测试，包含240个场景、585个角色实例和2340个角色任务实例，通过四种角色级任务评估MLLMs的视觉社交智能。实验发现MLLMs在局部角色扮演上接近饱和，但在交互调控和视觉化结果达成方面仍面临显著困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交智能基准大多基于文本，很少测试多模态agent能否利用面部表情、姿势、目光等视觉社交信号引导交互，缺乏对多模态视觉社交智能的系统评估。

Method: 构建了一个多模态社交模拟基准，包含240个场景、585个角色实例和2,340个角色任务实例，结合文本-视觉对齐证据、结构化角色档案，设计四种角色级任务（表情任务、特征任务、交互调控任务、交互结果任务）。在verbalized-vision和direct-vision两种模式下评估七个最新的MLLMs。

Result: 局部角色表达和冲突处理接近饱和；交互调控和基于视觉的结果达成任务仍然困难，存在显著能力差距。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在多模态社交智能中，角色层面的表达和冲突处理已近饱和，但交互层面的调控和视觉化结果推理仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Social interaction depends on both language and visible social signals, such as facial expressions, posture, gaze, and emotional shifts. Yet existing social-agent benchmarks are largely text-based and rarely test whether multimodal agents can use visual cues to guide interaction. We introduce \textsc{\benchmarkname{}}, a benchmark evaluating visual social intelligence in multimodal social simulation. It contains 240 scenarios, 585 role instances, and 2,340 role-task instances, combining aligned textual-visual evidence, structured role profiles, and four role-level tasks: expression task, characteristic task, interaction regulation task, and interaction outcome task. Evaluating seven recent MLLMs under verbalized-vision and direct-vision reveals a clear gap between local role enactment and interaction management: role-specific expression and conflict handling are near saturation, whereas interaction regulation and visually grounded outcome achievement remain substantially more difficult. The code is released at https://github.com/JunsWan/AgentViSS, and the dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/JunsWan/AgentViSS.

</details>


### [273] [Beyond Layer Importance in Layer-wise Sparsity: An Inter-Layer Perturbation-Absorption Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15161)
*Tao Jing,Ningxin Wu,Chen Kang,Dong Yu,Changliang Li,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控扰动实验发现LLM中不同层对剪枝扰动具有异质性响应：浅层放大扰动，中深层吸收扰动，且吸收现象仅在大扰动下出现。基于此定义吸收系数并提出吸收感知校正方法，在70%稀疏度下将OWL和AlphaPruning的困惑度降低7.13%，零样本准确率提升1.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非均匀剪枝方法主要基于局部信号（如激活异常值或权重谱）估计各层重要性，但忽略了剪枝后网络的补偿能力对最终性能的影响，本文旨在直接刻画这一特性。

Method: 通过受控扰动实验发现：（1）浅层放大扰动，中深层吸收扰动，相对L2漂移随深度单调递减且方向重新对齐；（2）吸收是大扰动现象，小扰动下网络全体放大，随扰动增大平滑过渡到吸收。基于此定义每层吸收系数，提出吸收感知校正方法作为正交增强模块。

Result: 在70%稀疏度下，吸收感知校正使OWL和AlphaPruning的困惑度降低7.13%，零样本准确率提升1.02%，跨多个模型家族有效。

Conclusion: 层对剪枝扰动的响应存在深层吸收机制，吸收系数可作为正交增强信号改善现有剪枝方法，且该发现丰富了相关工作中的线性化累积理论。

Abstract: The considerable layer-wise redundancy in large language models (LLMs) has established non-uniform sparsity allocation across layers as the standard pruning approach for efficient compression. Existing layer-wise allocation methods that estimate allocation strategy from local signals such as activation outliers or weight spectra mainly derive from local layer importance, whereas the final post-pruning performance is also influenced by the network's subsequent compensatory capacity. In this paper, we directly characterize this property through controlled perturbation experiments. We make the following empirical findings. First, layers exhibit highly heterogeneous responses to pruning-scale perturbations. In most cases, early layers amplify perturbations, while middle and late layers actively absorb them, with relative L2 drift decreasing monotonically across depth and direction realigning toward the unperturbed hidden-state trajectory. Second, absorption is a large-perturbation phenomenon. Under small perturbations the network exhibits amplification across all layers, and the transition to absorption occurs smoothly as perturbation magnitude grows to pruning scale. This enriches the linearized accumulation theory underlying related works. Building on these findings, we define an absorption coefficient per layer and propose absorption-aware correction, an orthogonal augmentation that improves OWL and AlphaPruning by reducing perplexity by 7.13% and boosting zero-shot accuracy by 1.02% across multiple model families at 70% sparsity.

</details>


### [274] [AmchiBias: Measuring Stereotypical Bias in Goan Identity Groups with a Minimal Pair Dataset in English and Konkani](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15191)
*Michelle Barbosa,Sebastian Padó,Franziska Weeber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AmchiBias，首个针对印度果阿地区社会文化刻板偏见评估的基准数据集，包含313个最小对，覆盖英语和天城文孔卡尼语。对五个多语言编码模型的评估发现：模型在孔卡尼语上表现接近随机（反映语言能力和果阿文化知识的双重缺失）；英语测试中，印度语言覆盖更强的模型对泛印度群体表现出更高偏见而非本地果阿群体，表明英语信号反映的是泛印度预训练关联而非真正的果阿文化知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP系统的偏见评估通常只关注国家层面，忽略了丰富的次国家社会文化结构。果阿具有独特的历史多元文化背景，但缺乏针对性的偏见评估基准。

Method: 构建AmchiBias基准数据集：涵盖313个最小对，涉及8个社会人口维度（如种姓、宗教、性别等），覆盖英语和天城文孔卡尼语两种语言。使用该基准评估5个多语言编码模型（如mBERT、XLM-R等）的刻板偏见。

Result: 1) 模型在孔卡尼语上表现接近随机，反映语言能力不足和果阿文化知识缺失；2) 英语查询中，印度语言覆盖更强的模型对泛印度群体的偏见高于对本地果阿群体的偏见，说明英语信号反映的是泛印度预训练关联而非果阿文化知识。

Conclusion: 低资源多语言NLP对超本地社区身份的评估存在关键缺口，现有模型缺乏对果阿等亚文化区域的文化能力。

Abstract: Socio-cultural stereotypical bias is an important consideration in the development and deployment of NLP systems. It is however often considered only at the national level, despite rich subnational socio-cultural structures. We present AmchiBias, the first benchmark for measuring socio-cultural stereotypical bias for the Indian state of Goa with its unique historically multicultural setting. It covers various Goan identity groups and comprises 313 minimal pairs across eight sociodemographic dimensions in both English and Devanagari Konkani. We then evaluate stereotypical bias in five multilingual encoder models on this benchmark. We find near-chance scores in Konkani, reflecting language incompetence for general multilingual models and a lack of Goan cultural competence for Indian language models. Queried in English, models with a stronger Indian language coverage show higher bias for pan-Indian groups than hyperlocal Goan groups. This suggests the English signal reflects pan-Indian pretraining associations rather than genuine Goan cultural knowledge. Our findings highlight a critical gap in low-resource multilingual NLP evaluation for hyperlocal community identities.

</details>


### [275] [Spokes: Optimizing for Diverse Pretraining Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15216)
*Clarence Lee,Yejin Choi,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh,Hai Leong Chieu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPOKES框架，通过G-Vendi分数和指数梯度下降直接优化数据多样性，在FineWeb和DCLM上分别提升下游性能+1.4和+1.5个点，优于语义去重和质量过滤等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样性是数据选择的关键因素，但作为集合级属性难以直接优化，现有代理或近似方法往往无法保证足够的子集多样性。

Method: 基于G-Vendi分数构建概率多样化框架，通过指数梯度下降直接优化多样性；联合优化质量和多样性得到最强结果。

Result: 在50万样本子集上G-Vendi分数提升+489；在DCLM和FineWeb上，仅多样性策略分别提升+0.4和+0.5个点，联合优化分别提升+1.5和+1.4个点，超越所有基线。

Conclusion: 直接优化多样性是有效的，且联合优化质量与多样性可获得最佳数据选择性能。

Abstract: Diversity plays a critical role in data selection, improving performance under fixed data budgets by reducing redundancy and repetition. However, optimizing for diversity is inherently challenging, as it is a set-level property that depends on interactions between data points rather than individual examples. As a result, existing approaches typically rely on proxies or approximations, which often fail to ensure sufficiently diverse subsets. In this work, we directly optimize diversity by introducing a probabilistic diversification framework based on the G-Vendi score, optimized via exponentiated gradient descent. Our method produces subsets that are substantially more diverse than those obtained via random sampling, achieving a +489 increase in G-Vendi score on a 500k-sample subset. We evaluate our approach on FineWeb and DCLM, where it consistently outperforms existing methods. Notably, SPOKES (diversity-only) improves average downstream performance by +0.4 and +0.5 points over random sampling on DCLM and FineWeb, respectively. More importantly, jointly optimizing for both quality and diversity yields the strongest results: SPOKES achieves gains of +1.5 and +1.4 points on DCLM and FineWeb, outperforming all baselines, including semantic deduplication and quality filtering.

</details>


### [276] [Evaluating and Preserving Lexical Stress in English-to-Chinese Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15266)
*Yuchen Song,Xi Chen,Mingze Li,Satoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对语音到语音翻译中词汇重音跨语言迁移这一未被充分探索的问题，构建了带重音标注的中文数据集和基于XLS-R的普通话重音检测器，提出了跨语言重音评估指标，并基于CosyVoice3构建了重音感知的S2ST系统，实验证明该方法在重音翻译能力上显著优于现有系统，且评估指标与人类主观判断高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有S2ST系统在语义准确性和语音自然度上取得了显著进展，但词汇重音（表达强调和说话者意图的关键线索）的跨语言迁移研究严重不足，且缺乏针对中文等声调语言的可靠自动评估指标。

Method: 构建了带重音标注的中文数据集；开发了基于XLS-R的普通话重音检测器；结合英语EmphAssess系统，提出了跨语言重音评估的客观指标；基于CosyVoice3微调构建了重音感知的S2ST系统。

Result: 提出的S2ST架构在重音翻译能力上显著优于现有系统，同时保持了具有竞争力的翻译质量；提出的评估指标与人类主观判断表现出强相关性。

Conclusion: 重音感知的S2ST系统能够有效实现跨语言重音迁移，所提出的评估指标可可靠地衡量这一能力。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) systems have achieved impressive progress in semantic accuracy and speech naturalness. However, the cross-lingual transfer of lexical stress, a vital cue for emphasis and speaker intent, remains heavily underexplored, compounded by a lack of reliable automatic evaluation metrics for tonal languages like Chinese. We investigate English-to-Chinese S2ST stress transfer by constructing a stress-annotated Chinese dataset and an XLS-R-based Mandarin stress detector. Integrating this with the English EmphAssess system, we propose a novel objective metric for cross-lingual stress evaluation. Furthermore, we fine-tune CosyVoice3 to build a stress-aware S2ST system. Experiments demonstrate that our proposed S2ST architecture significantly outperforms existing systems in stress translation capability while maintaining competitive translation quality. Furthermore, our evaluation metric exhibits a strong correlation with human subjective judgments.

</details>


### [277] [Adapting Reinforcement Learning with Chain-of-Thought Supervision for Explainable Detection of Hateful and Propagandistic Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15307)
*Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi,Mucahid Kutlu,Ali Ezzat Shahroor,Abul Hasnat,Firoj Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于强化学习（GRPO）的后训练方法，用于优化思考型多模态大语言模型在仇恨与宣传性模因内容审核中的分类性能与解释生成质量。通过在Hateful Memes和ArMeme基准上的实验，该方法在FHM准确率上提升达2.1%（至82.0%），在ArMeme宏F1上提升达7.6个点（至0.612），并能同时生成自然语言解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨与宣传性模因通过图像与文本的交互传达有害意图，单一模态无法揭示其完整含义。尽管思考型多模态大语言模型在视觉-语言理解方面取得进展，但其在模因内容审核中的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: (i) 系统性地评估现有MLLM在英文和阿拉伯文仇恨/宣传模因理解上的表现；(ii) 通过蒸馏和多LLM细粒度标注扩展现有模因数据集，添加弱监督思维链推理和宣传标注；(iii) 提出基于GRPO的目标函数，结合思考长度正则化，联合优化分类准确率和解释质量；(iv) 探索基于共识伪标签的自监督GRPO在无标注模因上的应用。

Result: 在Hateful Memes基准上FHM准确率从79.9%提升至82.0%（+2.1%）；在ArMeme基准上宏F1从0.536提升至0.612（+7.6点），同时生成自然语言解释。在ArMeme上，序列分类基线在原始准确率上仍更强，但本文方法提供了更均衡的每类性能及解释。

Conclusion: 基于GRPO的后训练方法有效提升了思考型MLLM在模因内容审核中的分类与解释能力，尤其在不平衡类别上表现更均衡，且可生成可解释性文本。代码、数据扩展和评估资源已公开。

Abstract: Hateful and propagandistic memes exploit the interplay between images and text to convey harmful intent that neither modality reveals alone. Although thinking-based multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced vision-language understanding, their application to meme content moderation remains underexplored. We propose a reinforcement learning-based post-training method that improves classification performance and reference-based explanation quality in thinking-based MLLMs via task-specific rewards and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Concretely, we (i) conduct a systematic empirical study of off-the-shelf MLLMs for hateful and propagandistic meme understanding across English and Arabic benchmarks, (ii) extend existing meme datasets with weakly supervised chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales via distillation and multi-LLM fine-grained propaganda annotations, (iii) introduce a GRPO-based objective with thinking-length regularization that jointly optimizes classification accuracy and explanation quality, and (iv) investigate self-supervised GRPO on unlabeled memes using consensus-based pseudo-labels. Experiments on the Hateful Memes and ArMeme benchmarks show that our approach improves over previously reported results on FHM accuracy (up to +2.1%, from 79.9% to 82.0%) and on ArMeme macro-F1 (up to +7.6 points, from 0.536 to 0.612 with explanations; +6.1 compared to the original ArMeme benchmark), while also generating natural-language explanations. On ArMeme, sequence-classification baselines remain stronger in terms of raw accuracy, whereas our approach provides more balanced per-class performance along with explanations. We publicly release our code, data extensions, and evaluation resources.

</details>


### [278] [Prior over Evidence: Stereotype-Driven Diagnosis in LLM-Based L2 Pronunciation Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15325)
*Rong Wang,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测试了三个音频大语言模型在1,800条L2-Arctic语料上对二语英语发音的评分能力。结果表明：评分准确性与基于证据的推理存在解耦（39.6%的推理虽内部一致但支持错误评分）；音素级反馈收敛于一个固定的L2英语困难音素清单，不受学习者母语背景或证据条件影响；声学证据仅在直接探测目标维度时提升评分（如文本化的F0范围提高音高变化接地性），而原始音频无法复现该改进。结论：当前通用LLM更适合作为外部发音证据的言语化工具，而非独立的诊断引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设LLM对L2英语发音的诊断基于提供的语音证据而非预训练先验知识，本文旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 使用1,800条L2-Arctic语料（覆盖6种L1背景），对3个音频能力LLM、4个发音维度、5种证据条件（从纯文本基线到数值声学特征再到原始音频）进行系统评估，每个细胞用评分准确性（RA）、证据一致性（EC）、接地正确性（GC）三个指标打分。

Result: 1）39.6%的单元含内部一致但支持错误评分的推理，仅15.8%支持正确评分；2）音素反馈收敛于固定L2英语困难音素清单；3）仅在文本化F0范围条件下音高变化接地性提升（0.18-0.19→0.45-0.62），原始音频无此改进；应激和音素正确性仍无法接地。

Conclusion: 当前通用LLM更可靠作为外部计算发音证据的言语化工具，而非独立诊断引擎；其评分准确性与接地推理存在系统性解耦。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed for written pronunciation feedback in second-language (L2) English learning, under the assumption that their diagnoses are grounded in the supplied speech evidence rather than in priors from pretraining. This assumption is tested on 1,800 L2-Arctic utterances spanning six L1 backgrounds, three audio-capable LLMs, four pronunciation dimensions, and five evidence conditions ranging from a text-only baseline to numeric acoustic features and raw audio. Each (utterance x model x condition x dimension) cell is scored on three metrics: Rating Accuracy (RA) against gold labels, Evidence Coherence (EC) assessing internal consistency without ground truth, and Grounded Correctness (GC) evaluated against gold evidence. Results show three findings across models. First, rating accuracy and grounded reasoning decouple: 39.6% of judged cells contain internally coherent reasoning that supports a wrong rating, against only 15.8% where the reasoning supports a correct rating. Second, phoneme-level feedback converges to a fixed inventory of L2-English difficulty phones that recurs across all six L1 backgrounds and all evidence conditions. Third, acoustic evidence improves the rating only when the supplied feature directly probes the target dimension: textualised F0 range raises pitch-variation grounding from (0.18-0.19) to (0.45-0.62) across all three models, while stress and phoneme correctness, which require target-to-realisation alignment, remain ungrounded. The same audio waveform without textualised F0 values does not reproduce this improvement. These findings indicate that current general-purpose LLMs are more reliable as verbalisers of externally computed pronunciation evidence than as standalone diagnostic engines.

</details>


### [279] [Replay What Matters: Off-Policy Replay for Efficient LLM Reinforcement Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15333)
*Zirui Pang,Chenlong Zhang,Haosheng Tan,Zhuoran Jin,Jiaheng Wei,Zixin Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM unlearning中on-policy RL训练效率低下的问题（易样本快速收敛而难样本仍产生低奖励但被丢弃），提出ReRULE方法，通过off-policy replay buffer存储低奖励难样本并在后续通过重要性采样复用，从而集中计算资源于边界难样本。理论证明更紧的收敛界，实验上在MUSE-Books上保留质量从46.3提升至56.2，仅增加5-11%训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL-based unlearning方法（如RULE）采用on-policy优化，反复从同一组forget/retain提示中采样，但易样本快速收敛后梯度信号微弱，而处于遗忘-保留边界的难样本产生的低奖励轨迹在一次使用后即被丢弃，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出ReRULE，在早期GRPO训练阶段将低奖励难样本的轨迹组存储到replay buffer中，后续阶段通过重要性采样的off-policy更新复用这些历史轨迹，从而将计算重新导向尚未学习好的边界样本。

Result: 理论上证明了ReRULE比纯on-policy RULE具有更紧的难样本收敛界；实验上在MUSE-Books上保留质量从46.3提升至56.2，仅增加5-11%训练时间；在较简单的TOFU任务上改进有限，验证了replay在难/易样本差异显著时最有效。

Conclusion: ReRULE通过off-policy replay机制有效提升了RL unlearning在难样本上的计算效率，在几乎不增加训练开销的前提下显著提升保留质量，且效果与任务难度异质性相关。

Abstract: LLM unlearning has emerged as a cost-effective alternative to full retraining for removing hazardous knowledge from pretrained models while preserving general utility. Recent RL-based methods such as RULE reformulate unlearning as learning a refusal behavior, but their on-policy optimization repeatedly samples from the same forget and retain/boundary prompts throughout training. We identify a critical inefficiency in this process: easy cases quickly converge and provide little useful gradient signal, while hard cases near the forget/retain boundary continue to produce low-reward rollouts that are discarded after a single use. To address this issue, we propose ReRULE, an off-policy replay enhancement for reinforcement unlearning. ReRULE stores low-reward hard-case rollout groups in a replay buffer during early GRPO training and reuses them in later stages through importance-sampled off-policy updates, redirecting computation toward boundary cases that still require learning. Theoretically, we show that ReRULE yields a tighter hard-case convergence bound than pure on-policy RULE. Empirically, ReRULE improves MUSE-Books Retain Quality from 46.3 to 56.2 while adding only 5--11% training time across benchmarks. Its limited improvement on the simpler TOFU setting further supports the intended conditional behavior: replay is most beneficial when the hard/easy disparity is pronounced.

</details>


### [280] [Privacy-Preserving Text Sanitization for Distributed Agents Collaboration via Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15335)
*Xuan Liu,Hefeng Zhou,Sicheng Chen,Chao Yang,Xingcheng Xu,Jingjing Qu,Jiong Lou,Jie LI,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DiSan框架，通过双流编码器将文本分解为任务相关的角色子空间和身份标识的风格子空间，结合联邦原型对齐与对抗正则化，在分布式多智能体协作中实现隐私保护，将PII暴露降低20倍，风格归因降低73.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式智能体跨组织交换文本时，隐私泄露不仅来自显式标识符，还来自分布特征（如格式习惯、词汇选择、句法模式），现有标识符级掩码方法不足以应对风格层面的隐私泄露。

Method: 采用双流编码器分解文本为源不变角色子空间（保留任务语义）和源识别风格子空间（本地保留），通过联邦原型对齐和对抗正则化实现联合训练，无需集中原始文本。

Result: 标识符级掩码（19.2% token）仅降低18.6% TF-IDF风格归因；DiSan将PII暴露降低20倍，保持83%答案忠实度，Enron风格归因降低73.2%（TF-IDF）和70.6%（神经探针）。

Conclusion: DiSan有效解决了分布级隐私泄露问题，超越了传统的标识符掩码方法，在隐私保护和任务效用之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: When distributed agents exchange text across organizational boundaries, privacy leakage arises not only from explicit identifiers but also from distributional signatures such as formatting conventions, vocabulary choices, and syntactic patterns. We propose DiSan(Disentangled Sanitization), a privacy-preserving sanitization framework and a built-in component of Intern-Shannon for multi-agent collaboration. DiSan uses a two-stream encoder to factorize text into a source-invariant role subspace that preserves task semantics and a source-identifying style subspace that remains local. Federated proto-type alignment and adversarial regularization enable joint training without centralizing raw text. Experiments show that identifier-level masking is insufficient: masking 19.2% of tokens reduces TF-IDF stylometric attribution by only 18.6%. By contrast, DiSan reduces answer-level PII exposure by 20 times while maintaining 83% answer faithfulness on a distributed multi-agent RAG benchmark, and lowers Enron stylometric attribution by 73.2% under TF-IDF and 70.6% under a neural probe.

</details>


### [281] [Beyond Monolingual Deep Research: Evaluating Agents and Retrievers with Cross-Lingual BrowseComp-Plus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15345)
*Yuheng Lu,Qingcheng Zeng,Heli Qi,Puxuan Yu,Fuheng Zhao,Rui Yang,Hitomi Yanaka,Naoto Yokoya,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了XBCP跨语言基准，用于测试深度研究智能体在证据语言与查询语言不一致时的表现。结果显示，即使使用强检索器，智能体在跨语言场景下的准确性、证据召回、校准性和引用可靠性均显著下降，且即使直接提供证据，准确率仍较低，表明存在独立的智能体端整合困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器基准大多假设用户查询与支持证据使用同一种语言，未考虑证据可能出现在另一种语言的情况，缺乏对跨语言场景下智能体搜索系统能力的评估。

Method: 构建XBCP基准，保留BrowseComp-Plus的英文问答空间，但改变支持文档的语言。设置两个场景：跨语言场景（每个查询对应单一语言证据）和多语言场景（证据语料均匀分布在12种高/低资源语言中）。评估四种深度研究智能体，使用稀疏和稠密多语言检索器，测量准确率、证据召回、搜索行为、校准性、引用忠实度和Oracle检索。

Result: 跨语言场景下性能显著下降：强稠密检索器也会损失证据召回，智能体校准性变差，引用可靠性降低。即使直接提供所有黄金证据，准确率仍较低。

Conclusion: 跨语言深度研究暴露了检索失败和智能体端在整合语言不匹配证据时的独立困难，表明当前系统在处理跨语言证据时存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Deep research agents are increasingly evaluated on their ability to search for evidence, reason over retrieved sources, and produce grounded answers. Existing browsing benchmarks, however, largely assume that the user's query and the supporting evidence are written in the same language, leaving open whether agentic search systems can operate when relevant evidence appears in another language. We introduce XBCP (Cross-lingual BrowseComp-Plus), a controlled benchmark that preserves the English question-and-answer space of BrowseComp-Plus but varies the languages of the supporting documents. XBCP instantiates two complementary settings: in the cross-lingual setting, each query is paired with evidence in a single assigned language. In the multilingual setting, the full evidence corpus is distributed equally and randomly across 12 languages spanning high-resource and low-resource regimes. We evaluate four deep research agents using sparse and dense multilingual retrievers, measuring answer accuracy, evidence recall, search behavior, calibration, citation fidelity, and oracle retrieval. Results reveal substantial degradation when evidence is translated. Even strong, dense retrievers lose evidence recall, and agents become less calibrated and cite evidence less reliably. Notably, accuracy remains lower even when all gold evidence is supplied directly. These findings suggest that cross-lingual deep research exposes both retrieval failures and an independent, agent-side difficulty in integrating language-mismatched evidence.

</details>


### [282] [Rethinking the Role of Efficient Attention in Hybrid Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15378)
*Ziqing Qiao,Yinuo Xu,Chaojun Xiao,Zhou Su,Zihan Zhou,Yingfa Chen,Xiaoyue Xu,Xu Han,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了混合架构中高效注意力模块（如滑动窗口注意力SWA和循环序列混合器）对模型能力的影响，从缩放行为、机制分析和架构设计三个角度展开。研究发现：高效注意力设计主要影响长上下文能力的涌现速度，不同混合架构在充分训练下最终能达到相近的长上下文性能；长距离检索主要由全注意力层完成，而高效注意力影响其优化轨迹，解释了"大窗口惰性"现象；基于此机制，对小型窗口SWA混合架构的全注意力层应用NoPE能显著提升长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型越来越多地采用全注意力与高效注意力模块（如SWA和循环序列混合器）的混合架构，但这些高效模块如何塑造模型能力仍缺乏深入理解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从三个视角进行系统分析：1) 缩放行为：研究不同混合架构在长上下文能力上的涌现速度与收敛表现；2) 机制分析：通过实验发现长距离检索主要由全注意力承担，高效注意力影响其优化轨迹，并揭示"大窗口惰性"现象（更大的SWA窗口会延迟全注意力层中检索头的形成）；3) 架构设计：基于机制洞察，提出对小型窗口SWA混合的全注意力层应用NoPE（无位置编码）的方法。

Result: 1) 高效注意力设计主要影响长上下文能力的涌现速度，不同混合架构在充分训练后性能趋同；2) 发现了"大窗口惰性"现象：更大的SWA窗口会延迟全注意力层中检索头的形成；3) 对小型窗口SWA混合的全注意力层应用NoPE，能在几乎不影响短上下文性能的情况下显著提升长上下文性能。

Conclusion: 混合架构中高效注意力模块的设计选择主要影响长上下文能力的学习速度而非最终性能上限；全注意力层与高效注意力层在混合架构中有明确的功能分工；基于机制理解可以指导更优的混合架构设计。

Abstract: Modern language models increasingly adopt hybrid architectures that combine full attention with efficient attention modules, such as sliding-window attention (SWA) and recurrent sequence mixers. However, how these efficient modules shape model capabilities remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we conduct a systematic analysis across hybrid architectures from three perspectives: scaling behavior, mechanism analysis, and architecture design. First, from a scaling perspective, we find that efficient-attention design primarily affects how fast long-context capability emerges, while different hybrids eventually converge to comparable long-context performance under sufficient training. Second, mechanistically, we show that long-range retrieval is mainly carried by full attention, whereas efficient attention shapes its optimization trajectory. This explains a counter-intuitive phenomenon we call Large-Window Laziness: larger SWA windows can delay the formation of retrieval heads in full-attention layers. Third, guided by this mechanism, we show that applying NoPE to only the full-attention layers of a small-window SWA hybrid substantially improves long-context performance with negligible impact on short-context performance.

</details>


### [283] [Not All Skills Help: Measuring and Repairing Agent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15390)
*Yixuan Wang,Yiyang Zhou,Yiming Liang,Congyu Zhang,Fuxiao Liu,Jiawei Zhou,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASSAY框架，通过将技能生成与技能策划分离开来，利用每个技能在开发集上的因果归因来抑制对特定测试任务有负面影响的技能，从而显著提升LLM代理性能，在AppWorld和tau-bench基准上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理系统将技能生成与技能策划分混淆，完全依赖LLM自身的判断来决定保留哪些技能以及如何应用，但忽视了单个技能对不同类型的任务可能有正向或负向的因果影响，且这些效果在全局聚合时会相互抵消，导致全局筛选方法无法有效识别和处置有负面影响的技能。

Method: 提出ASSAY框架：（1）通过随机掩码测量每个技能在开发集上的因果贡献；（2）离线重构技能库；（3）在推理时，对每个测试任务抑制预测有负面影响的技能。核心创新在于将技能生成与策划分离开，用经验证据而非LLM主观判断来指导技能筛选。

Result: 跨越7个基座模型、4个供应商和2个基准（AppWorld和tau-bench），ASSAY一致优于先前的技能策划分方法。在AppWorld最难子集上，DeepSeek-V3达到69.3%任务完成率（相对提升47.4%），超越所有已发表方法（包括权重微调方法）。在tau-bench零售场景，GPT-4.1相对提升8.7%，无权重修改即超越o4-mini、o1和GPT-4.5。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实主要增益来自于每个任务的掩码机制，说明瓶颈在于推理时将技能与任务匹配，而非全局移除坏技能。这挑战了现有方法依赖LLM判断进行全局技能筛选的范式，强调基于经验因果证据的细粒度技能策划分的重要性。

Abstract: LLM agents can improve without weight updates by accumulating natural-language skills from experience, but current systems entrust every decision about which skills to keep and how to apply them to LLM judgment alone. We argue that this conflates two distinct roles: generating a skill from experience is a creative act that judgment handles well, while deciding whether that skill actually helps requires empirical evidence across many tasks.
